INJECTOR PATH TRACE -- from 0x0301E4's outputs [0xFFFF7320]..[0xFFFF7344] to the injector pulse register
=================================================================================================================
date            2026-11-27
reference bin   rom/AE5L600L 20g rev 20.19c.bin   md5 92cae8275cd4f9b473a3a9e36efe6449
                (stock rom/ae5l600l.bin md5 a8ea39d447f977e270e27ee670243c88 and rom/AE5L600L 20g rev 20.19e.bin
                md5 a249be081121f49f20ae0b32f079f114: every code and calibration byte used below is identical, pass Q)
method          blind fn_dump of the six entries and every function on the forward path, readers by an EA scan and a literal
                search, identities only from definition names; disputes settled by a third decode (REVIEW HISTORY).
EVIDENCE CLASS  bytes + emulated: scripts/trace/emu_injector_path.py, 30 functions run against models written from the
                bytes (passes A-V, 180,838 assertions, exit 0; every instruction of 26 of them executed; 0x03D856 221 of
                328 -- its [7E55] == 1 initialisation block is not run; 0x037B74 107 of 111; 0x03D0B8 99 of 104 -- the
                other five are dead).  Pass R runs the whole chain 0x0301E4 -> ... -> 0x011848 with every function
                REAL (only the critical-section helpers stubbed).  Definition names: "Per Injector Pulse Width
                Compensation A-D", "Injector Latency_", "Injector Flow Scaling" (data pointers), axes "Last Calculated
                Base Pulse Width", "Engine Speed", "Battery Output", "Manifold Pressure".  The runtime library and
                0x0301E4 / 0x02FFE0 / 0x03D2FC / 0x03DCB6 are used through their verified CONTRACTs (helpers_*_trace.txt,
                rtos_core_trace.txt, cl_target_to_fuel_trace.txt, ffb_residual_trace.txt, launch_closure_trace.txt).
                The hardware module at 0xFFFFF440.. is NOT named (no hardware manual is cited here); the peripheral
                ADDRESSES are from bytes.

ANSWER
  The pulse the injector gets is a per-channel u16 written to the peripheral register 0xFFFFF640 + 2j (j = 0..3), and
  its start is the compare value written to 0xFFFFF444 + 2j.  For channel j, and for a FRESH pulse -- a MODE-1 record
  whose counts 0x03DFC8 hands to 0x009B2C, armed by 0x009F72 with status bit j of u16 [0xFFFFF666] clear (the path
  pass R runs end to end with every function real):

      PW_j      = [7290 + 4j]                                        (f32, written by 0x02EFD2)
                = [7328 + 4j]  once the after-start floor has released (no byte of [726F]..[7272] equal to 1)
                = clamp( [7310 + 4j] * (S4 * C) * [7B6C + 4j] * F , [733C] , 262136.0 )      (0x0301E4, verified)
      [733C]    = 0.0 if byte [641C] == 1, else [0x0CBE08] = 300.0 (all three bins): written by 0x02FF8E (02FFA0, its
                  only writer found) at 02FF7E, immediately before 0x0301E4 in the same 0x02FF7C call (AUDIT, pass V6;
                  ffb_residual_trace.txt).  So every 0x0301E4 output is >= 0.0, and >= 300.0 unless [641C] == 1.
      C         = ([76D4] logged AFC + [7878] logged AFL + [7AE4]) * [7348]
      [7348]    = clamp( [63F8] * 4916.0 ("Injector Flow Scaling" raw) , 0 , 131072 )         (0x030378, verified)
      [7310+4j] = clamp( (S + [7B44 + 4j] + [7A78 + 4j]) * [7340] , 0 , 32 )  per-cylinder FFB (0x02FFE0, verified)
      [7B6C+4j] = "Per Injector Pulse Width Compensation A/B/C/D"( [7324] , [6624] )  (0x038DC0; factor 0.898..1.086)
      F = [7BC4]= clamp( T(AC760, [7344]) , 0.5 , 1.5 ) when 4000.0 > [6624] and 600.0 > [7344], else 1.0  (0x0397AC;
                  1.265625 at [7344] <= 300 falling to 1.0 at 600; [7344] = clamp(C * [730C], [733C], 262136))
      S4 = [7AB4] = clamp(1 + [7ABC]*[7AC0]*[7AC4]*[7AC8], 0.5, 1.5) or 1.0 (0x037B74).  THREE of the four factors are
                  0.0 in all three bins: [7ABC] (AD71C, through 0x037D74), [7AC4] = T(AC634, [6624]) and [7AC8] =
                  T(AC648, [6350]).  [7AC0] is written only by 0x037E70, as 0.0 / 1.0 / min([7AC0], 1.0) / max([7AC0],
                  0.0) (its steps 0x0CC32C / 0x0CC330 are 0.0), so it holds.  S4 = 1.0 unless [7AC0] holds +inf or
                  -inf, which no path makes from a finite value (pass T); its value before the first write is not
                  established.
      counts_j  = trunc( 4 * PW_j * [7DA4] ) ,  [7DA4] = 1.0           (0x03D582 -> record j +24; 0x03D68C)
      register  = min( (counts_j + L) >> 4 , 0xFFFF )  -> u16 [0xFFFFF640 + 2j], written twice; the excess w - 0xFFFF
                  goes to [4294 + 28j]                   (0x03DFC8 -> 0x009B2C -> 0x009F72 -> 0x00A4E0 -> 0x011848)
      L = [4280]= u16 "Injector Latency_"( [4130] "Battery Output" axis , [6210] "Manifold Pressure" axis )  (0x0303C0)

  NOT covered by that formula: (a) counts arriving while the channel's pulse is in flight (sub+1 == 1): 0x03DFC8 sends
  them to 0x009C38, which rewrites the running width as remaining + ((P' - drec+12) >> 4) (bytes only); (b) 0x009F72's
  status-set branch (00A062..00A130), which computes a different width and never writes drec+20 to the compare
  register; (c) MODE 0 and handler B (not decoded).

  Arithmetic (pass R): 0 <= 4*PW + L - 16*register < 16 while 4*PW + L <= 0xFFFFF.  Beyond that the register is 0xFFFF
  and the excess sits in [4294 + 28j]: at the 262136 ceiling 167 / 785 counts for L = 2693 / 12588 (the 0xFFFF edge
  is PW 261470.5 at L = 2693 and 258996.25 at L = 12588).  0x00A55E (register + excess) is called by 0x009F72,
  0x009C38, 0x00A280 and 0x00A470 (census); how the timer or an interrupt consumes the excess is NOT established.
  Units: the definitions give [7324]'s axis the scaling x*0.001 ms and the latency raw x*0.00025 ms; adding 4*PW to the
  latency raw is consistent with both, and then one register count = 16 latency counts = 4 us.  That holds only if both
  definition scalings are right -- the timer clock is NOT established from bytes.  The latency table is flat across
  [6210]: 3.147 / 1.714 / 1.125 / 0.806 / 0.673 ms (definition scaling) at 6.5 / 9 / 11.5 / 14 / 16.5 on [4130].

  What is added or multiplied AFTER 0x0301E4: only (1) the after-start floor of 0x02EFD2: while ANY flag byte
  [726F..7272] is 1, EVERY channel gets [7328 + 4j] if that exceeds the decaying floor [728C], else [728C] -- a channel
  whose flag is already 0 is floored again if its pulse drops below [728C] (AUDIT, pass V1); the floor is gone only
  once all four flags are 0 (every factor of the eight decay tables is 0.85..0.96 in all three bins, so [728C] falls
  geometrically), (2) the factor [7DA4] = 1.0 and truncation to quarter units,
  (3) the latency L, added in the driver, (4) per-channel suppression by bit j of u16 [7D80] (0x03D2FC) through byte
  rec_j+1:
      - 0x03D856's per-record loop skips 0x03D582 and handler A for a record whose rec+1 != 0;
      - rec+1 is set OR cleared from the bit at the record's re-arm (tooth angle == phase, 03DA3A), by 0x03DB16
        (03DB88; called when the tooth step != 1), and AT ONCE by 0x03DE6E when 0x03D856 sees [7D80] differ from its
        copy [7E52]: set when the record is idle (rec+2 == 0) or can be cancelled (rec+2 == 1 and its sub-record is
        neither in state 2 nor in flight with [429B+28k] == 0; then 0x009D48), cleared when rec+2 == 1, or rec+2 == 0
        and handler B returns 1;
      - EXCEPTION: 0x03DB16 runs 0x03D582 and handler B (0x03E26E / 0x03E0BA, neither decoded) for EVERY record,
        suppressed or not.  So "a suppressed channel gets no pulse" is established for the 0x03D856 loop only.
  The per-cylinder trims ARE in 0x0301E4 ([7B6C+4j], computed one 0x04AA58 pass earlier from the previous [7324]);
  the per-cylinder FFB values [7310+4j] are read by 0x0301E4 ONLY (no other reader, no literal of 0xFFFF7310..731C
  anywhere in the image).

  The other outputs do not enter the pulse of the SAME pass: [7344] -> 0x0397AC only (it builds F for the next pass);
  [7320] -> 0x037D74 only (-> [7ABC] = 0.0 -> S4); [7338] -> 0x03D0B8 only (an AFC-like ratio [7324]/[7338] - [7878] -
  [7AE4] and three lags at 0xFFFF7D58..7D64); [7324] -> 0x038DC0 (the PIPWC axis), 0x03D0B8 and 0x0345A4; [7328]..
  [7334] -> 0x02EFD2 (the pulse), 0x07D620 (a diagnostic gate: all four > [733C]; writes only 0xFFFFA290..A29C) and
  0x04BDAA (a DEAD load: no output depends on it, emulated).  An audit re-scan that also resolves pointer-table loads
  finds no other reader of [7320]..[7344].
  BUT two of them reach 0x03452A (runner 0x049CF0), the A/F-learning pass that by afl_learning_trace.txt (same batch,
  NOT used as evidence here) writes [7878], a term of C; find_writers itself shows only the 0x034488 seed (AUDIT):
      - [7324]: 0x0345A4 (called at 03456C) sets byte [7883] = 1 only if nine terms hold, one of them [7324] > [733C]
        (034756, strict; NaN fails) -- false exactly when [7324] sits on its lower clamp (pass V3);
      - [7338]: 0x03D0B8's lags [7D5C] / [7D64] are inputs of 0x03CD4A's [7D68] (03CF46..03CF54, through 0x03D23A,
        not decoded), and 0x03452A copies [7D68] into [7874] (03457E..034586).
  Whether [7883] or [7874] changes [7878] -- i.e. whether [7324] / [7338] feed back into a LATER pulse -- is NOT
  established here (0x03452A's callees are not decoded in this file).  The earlier "not on the pulse path" for [7338]
  and the [7D5C..7D64] lags is withdrawn.

  Start mode: byte [726C] == 1 gives MODE 0 (0x03D4F6 -> 0x063DFC[0] = 0), whose pulse comes from 0x02F9E0 =
  clamp([7300] + A*[72E0]*[72E4]*B*[72F4]*[72E8], 0, 262136), not from 0x0301E4's outputs; its handler A 0x03E168 is
  not decoded (by the census it reaches 0x009F72 through 0x009A72 / 0x009C38).  A record's MODE byte rec+0 is written
  at its re-arm (03DA20), by 0x03DB16 (03DB6E), and AT ONCE by 0x03DD8E (03DE4E) when 0x03D856 sees 0x03D4F6's result
  change -- for a record more than 100 deg from its phase that is idle (rec+2 == 0), or scheduled (rec+2 == 1) when
  handler B of the OLD mode returns 1 and the sub-record can be cancelled.  After init (0x00DF14 -> 0x047C40 ->
  0x02EF08 -> 0x02F85E -> 0x02F83E) byte [726C] = 1, bytes [726F..7272] = 1 and the latch [72D1] = 0.
  0x02F83E is NOT init-only (AUDIT, pass V2): the task-9 runner 0x04907C, on every pass its gate byte [8EDC] is 0
  (straight line 0490E8 .. 0491B0; revlim_freeze_gate_trace.txt), calls 0x02EF0C, which calls 0x02F83E when byte
  [65F6] == 1 (0x022F92).  That re-selects MODE 0 and the floor RESET for the next 0x02EFD2 pass, sets the four flags
  and clears the latch [72D1] at run time.  When [65F6] is 1 after start is NOT established ([65F6] = byte [415C],
  copied by 0x022F1C (022F2C..022F30; callers 0x047C40 / 0x048E16) and zeroed by 0x022DB0 (022DC2..022DC6; caller
  0x04A94C) -- the only two writers find_writers reports, re-read here from fn_dump).

  The logged IPW (logcfg P21, id 0x000020, scaling x*256/1000 ms): getter index 32 = 0x05D478 returns clamp(trunc(
  [7D84]/256 + 0.5), 0, 255), where [7D84] = 0.25 * record-0 counts + [734C] (0x03D648), [734C] = L / 4, and 0.0 while
  rec_0+1 != 0.  IF paramid 0x20 selects getter index 32, the logged value is record 0's scheduled pulse plus latency
  rounded to 256 PW units.  That index = paramid is NOT proven: the writer of the index u16 [8F28] is not decoded
  (index 9 -> 0x05D2C0 reading [76D4] with the logged AFC id 0x09 is consistent, not proof).

  Calibration: every table and scalar above is byte-identical in stock, 20.19c and 20.19e (pass Q): Injector Flow
  Scaling 4916.0; latency 12588 / 6857 / 4499 / 3225 / 2693 raw; PIPWC A-D as printed in DESCRIPTORS; AC760; AD71C,
  AC634, AC648 (zero); 0x0CC32C / 0x0CC330 (0.0); 4000.0 / 600.0 gates.  [0x0CBDB4] = 30800 enters only angle spans:
  as [42F4] (0x03D640 -> 0x009E44) in 0x009B2C and 0x009EA0, and read directly by 0x03E0FA; never the width.

  NOT established: which physical cylinder record j drives (record j has phase 0 / 360 / 180 / 540 degrees and driver
  channel j); the SH7058 module behind 0xFFFFF440..; the timer clock (the "4 us" is from definition scalings); what
  consumes the excess in [4294 + 28j]; the in-flight path 0x009C38, handler B (0x03E26E / 0x03E0BA), handler A of
  MODE 0 and the asynchronous path 0x03DCB6 -> 0x00A280 in full; the power-on value of [7AC0], [8374], [8375];
  whether [7324] / [7338] change [7878] through the A/F-learning pass; when byte [65F6] is 1 after start.

ABBREVIATIONS  [XXXX] = f32 at 0xFFFFXXXX unless "byte" / "u16" / "long" is written; rec_j = 0xFFFF7DA8 + 40 j (j = 0..3,
  the scheduler records); drec_k = 0xFFFF4284 + 28 k (the driver records).  T(D, x) = 0x0BE830 1-D lookup, T2(D, a, b)
  = 0x0BE8E4 2-D lookup with fr4 = a on the axis at record +4 and fr5 = b on the axis at record +8 (verified; both clamp
  at the axis ends, linear between).  clamp = 0x0BE56C (NaN -> lo).  lag = 0x0BEA40: new + (1 - k)(old - new), snap.
T-BIT SENSES RELIED ON
  fcmp/gt FRm,FRn  T = (FRn > FRm), false with NaN.  e.g. F985 at 0397BA: T = (fr9 > fr8) = (4000.0 > [6624]);
                   F895 at 02F180: T = (fr8 > fr9) = ([7328] > [728C]); F955 at 03D0F8: T = (fr9 > fr5) = (4.0 > [7338])
                   F695 at 034756 (audit): T = (fr6 > fr9) = ([7324] > [733C])
  cmp/hs Rm,Rn     T = (Rn >= Rm) unsigned.  3622 at 034732 / 034752: T = (r6 >= r2) on two sign-extended bytes, which
                   keeps unsigned byte order (audit)
  cmp/ge Rm,Rn     T = (Rn >= Rm) signed.  3263 at 037D90: T = (r2 >= r6) = (unsigned byte [7ADD] >= 2)
  cmp/hi Rm,Rn     T = (Rn > Rm) unsigned.  3E46 at 00A4F0: T = (r14 > r4) = (width > 0xFFFF)
  cmp/eq #imm,r0   T = (r0 == imm) on a sign-extended byte: only 0x01 equals 1
  tst Rm,Rn        T = ((Rn & Rm) == 0); bt/bf branch on T = 1 / 0; bt/s, bf/s run the slot on both paths
  shar             arithmetic shift right (0x009F72 width: >> 4 by four shar); shlr2 logical
  sh2e_disasm prints displacements already in bytes.

DESCRIPTORS (decoded from the records; name only where a definition names the DATA pointer)

RAM CELLS WHOSE WRITER IS DECODED HERE (neutral handles)
; [7290+4j]   PW_j, the per-channel pulse (us per the axis scaling)        0x02EFD2
; [728C]      after-start floor, decays by [7274] per pass                  0x02EFD2   [7274] decay factor 0x02EFD2
; byte [726F+j] floor-active flag of channel j (1 -> 0 when PW beats floor)  0x02EFD2 / 0x02F83E (sets all 1)
; byte [72D1] latch, 1 once byte [7306] == 1                               0x02EFD2 (0 only from 0x02F83E, 02F85C;
;             0x02F83E runs at init: 0x00DF14 -> 0x047C40 -> 0x02EF08 -> 0x02F85E, pass U; AND at run time from
;             task-9 runner 0x04907C -> 0x02EF0C when byte [65F6] == 1, pass V2)
; [733C]      0.0 if byte [641C] == 1 else 300.0, the lower clamp of 0x0301E4  0x02FF8E (audit, pass V6)
; [7B6C+4j]   per-injector compensation factor j                           0x038DC0
; [7BC4] F, [7BC8] its unclamped table value                               0x0397AC
; [7ACC] copy of [7320], [7ABC] AD71C output (0.0)                          0x037D74
; [7AB4] S4                                                                 0x037B74 (run whole, pass T)
; [7AC0] the held S4 factor: 0.0 / 1.0 / min(old, 1.0) / max(old, 0.0)      0x037E70 (the only writer found)
; [7D58] clamp([7324]/[7338] - [7878] - [7AE4], 0, 2) or 2.0 / 1.0; [7D5C] [7D60] [7D64] its lags   0x03D0B8
;             (all four := 1.0 at init by 0x03CD00, caller 0x047C40 -- audit, pass V4)
; byte [7883] = 1 iff nine byte / float terms incl. [7324] > [733C]           0x0345A4 second body (audit, pass V3)
; [8358] [835C] [8360], bytes [8374] [8375] [8368] [8369]                   0x04BDAA
; long rec_j+24  counts_j = trunc(4 * PW_j * [7DA4])                        0x03D582
; long rec_j+20  counts handed to the driver (copy of rec_j+24)             0x03DFC8
; [7DA4] = 1.0                                                              0x03D68C
; [7D94+4j] = 0.25 * rec_j+20, [7D84+4j] = that + [734C]                    0x03D648
; u16 [7350] = latency raw, long [4280] = same, [734C] = raw / 4            0x0303C0 (+ 0x009E4A); [4280] = 0
;             earlier, from 0x009860 at init (009922)
; long [42F4] = [0x0CBDB4] = 30800                                          0x03D640 -> 0x009E44; = 0 from 0x009860
;             (009926) at init, before it
; long drec_k+0 = counts P, +4 start angle, +8 span                         0x009B2C

==== FUNCTION 0x02EFD2 .. 0x02F22A  (237 instructions, 1 caller: tail branch from 0x02EF4C, called by 0x04AA58 at 04AB22) ====

CONTRACT
  Reads byte [7306], [726C], [726F]..[7272], [3158], [90C1], [72D1]; f32 [7328]..[7334], [6354], [728C], [72DC].
  Writes [7290]..[729C] (PW_0..PW_3) on every call, plus the cells named below; nothing else outside the stack.
    first:   byte [7306] == 1  ->  byte [72D1] = 1          (a latch: this function never stores 0 there)
    RESET    byte [726C] != 0:  [728C] = [72DC];  [7290+4j] = [728C];  bytes [726F+j] = 1       (j = 0..3)
    FLOOR    else, if any byte [726F+j] == 1:
               D = one of eight 1-D records chosen by (byte [90C1] != 0, byte [3158] != 0, byte [72D1] == 1)
               [7274] = T(D, [6354]);  [728C] = max([728C] * [7274], 0.0)
               per j: if [7328+4j] > [728C]:  byte [726F+j] = 0;  [7290+4j] = [7328+4j]
                      else                 :  [7290+4j] = [728C]
    COPY     else: [7290+4j] = [7328+4j]   for j = 0..3
  The per-channel compare on the FLOOR path does NOT test the channel's own flag (02F17A..02F1E4 read only [7328+4j]
  and [728C]): while any flag is 1, a channel whose flag is already 0 still gets [728C] whenever its pulse is not above
  it (AUDIT, pass V1: flags 0/1/1/1, pulses 1000 / 6000 x3, [728C] 5000 at 90 deg -> PW_0 = 4499.969 = the decayed
  floor, not 1000).  A flag goes 1 -> 0 the first time its channel beats the floor; the floor stops only when all four
  flags are 0, after which the pulse passes straight through.  The flags are set back to 1 only by RESET here and by
  0x02F83E (which also sets byte [726C] = 1 and stores byte [72D1] = 0 at 02F85C).  0x02F83E runs at init (0x00DF14
  at 00E10A -> 0x047C40 at 047F04 -> 0x02EF08 -> 0x02F85E at 02F888; listings in the 0x009860 section; emulated, pass
  U), so after init the first call takes the RESET path, the four flags are 1 and the latch [72D1] is 0.  It ALSO runs
  at run time: task-9 runner 0x04907C -> 0x02EF0C (0491B0) -> 0x02F83E when byte [65F6] == 1 (AUDIT section below;
  pass V2), so the floor, the flags and the latch can be re-armed after start.

  -60 7294, -56 7298, -52 729C.  NaN [7328+4j] is not > [728C]: the floor is stored and the flag stays.  A NaN
  product in max() gives 0.0 (0x0BE960 returns fr5 when fr4 is not greater).  Table choice:

==== FUNCTION 0x03D648 .. 0x03D68C  (34 instructions, 1 caller: 0x04A94C at 04A9D4) -- writer of the logged IPW cell ====

CONTRACT

==== EXCERPTS of FUNCTION 0x009860 .. 0x00993C  (110 instructions, 1 caller 0x00DF14 at 00DFFE) and 0x009EA0 .. 0x009F1C (62, 2 callers) -- the init order; the other writer / reader of [4280] [42F4] ====

CONTRACT
  0x009860 (driver init): for k = 0..3: bytes drec_k+23 = drec_k+24 = 0; 0x00E6E4(0xFFFFF66C, 1 << k, 0); u16
  [0xFFFFF640 + 2k] = 0 through 0x011848 (stored twice).  Then a peripheral set-up (0098A6..00991E, not listed) and
  [4280] = 0, [42F4] = 0.  0x00DF14 calls it at 00DFFE, BEFORE 0x047C40 at 00E10A, which reaches 0x0303C0 (047EFE),
  0x03D708 -> 0x03D640 (04864C) and 0x02EF08 -> 0x02F85E -> 0x02F83E (047F04).  So 30800 and the latency are the values
  after init; before them both cells are 0 and every width register starts at 0.
  0x009EA0(k), only when byte [429B + 28k] == 2: re-computes the span (drec_k+0 + [42F4] + [4280]) * 180 / [4158] and
  moves the start angle by the change (009EB2..009F00; the rest is not listed).  It is the second reader of [42F4].

==== EXCERPTS of FUNCTION 0x03D856 .. 0x03DB16  (328 instructions, 1 caller 0x04A94C at 04A9CE) -- the scheduler pass ====

CONTRACT  (r4 = the task argument, the byte 0x04A94C saved; r8 = 0xFFFF7E48 literal; rec_j = 0xFFFF7DA8 + 40 j with
           one 24-byte sub-record sub = rec+16; k = byte sub+0 = j)
  step = (r4 - byte [7E54]) mod 24;  byte [7E54] = r4;  tooth angle T = 30 * (r4 < 24 ? r4 : r4 - 24) deg (Q16.16)
  byte [7E55] == 1: an initialisation block (03D89E..03D972, not analysed) runs instead of the refresh calls below.
  else:  step != 1                    -> 0x03DB16 (below: refreshes every record and both copies)
         0x03D4F6() != byte [7E51]    -> 0x03DD8E(T, 0x063DFC[new]) (below), then byte [7E51] = new
         u16 [7D80] != u16 [7E52]     -> 0x03DE6E(T, new mask) (below),      then u16 [7E52] = new
  then for each record, with d = 0x03DD80(T, rec+8) = phase - T, plus 720 deg unless that is > 0:
    RE-ARM only when d >= 720<<16 (signed), i.e. when T equals the record phase (0 / 360 / 180 / 540 deg, 0x063E20):
        sub+1 = 0, rec+2 = 0, rec+7 = 0, rec+0 = 0x063DFC[byte [7E51]], rec+1 = bit j of u16 [7E52] (set or
        clear), rec+3 = 0
    r12 = 0x063E00 + 16 * rec+0 (long 720<<16, long 100<<16, handler A, handler B; the same limits for both MODEs)
    rec+2 == 0:  skip while byte [429B + 28k] != 0;  if !(d > 100<<16): rec+2 = 2, skip;  skip if d > 720<<16;
                 if rec+3 == 0: u16 [7E48] = sat_add_u16([7E48], 1), u16 rec+4 = [7E48], rec+3 = 1;
                 skip if rec+1 != 0;  0x03D50E(rec);  sub+12 = sub+16;  0x03D582(rec);  handler A(rec, d)
    rec+2 == 1:  skip if rec+1 != 0;  skip unless byte rec+6 == 1;  0x03D582(rec);  handler A(rec, d)
    other:       skip
  Handler A = long 0x063E08 + 16 * MODE: 0x03E168 (MODE 0, not decoded) / 0x03DFC8 (MODE 1).  0x04A94C itself returns
  at once while byte [8EDC] != 0 (04A950..04A956).
  So in THIS loop a record with rec+1 != 0 gets neither 0x03D582 nor handler A (the [7E48] count still runs).  rec+0
  and rec+1 are latched bytes, rewritten at the re-arm, by 0x03DB16, and at once on a change by 0x03DD8E / 0x03DE6E.
  So bit j of [7D80] (verified launch_closure CONTRACT of 0x03D2FC: 0xFFFF on rev-limit cut) suppresses channel j in
  this loop.  What writes byte rec+6, byte [429B + 28k] and byte [7E55] is not traced here.
EVIDENCE
  03D86C..03D896 step and angle; 03D898..03D89C; 03D974..03D9BA the refresh calls; 03D9F0..03DA3E re-arm; 03DA40..03DAEA
  the two paths.  emulated: pass S4 (500 cases; 0x03DB16 / 0x03DD8E / 0x03DE6E / 0x03D50E / 0x03D582 / both handlers

==== FUNCTION 0x03DB16 .. 0x03DBF4  (111 instructions, 1 caller: 0x03D856 at 03D97C) -- refresh every record ====

CONTRACT
  T = 30 * (byte [4167], minus 24 once if >= 24) deg (Q16.16) -- from [4167], not from 0x03D856's argument.
  byte [7E51] = 0x03D4F6();  u16 [7E52] = u16 [7D80];  then for EVERY record j, with d = 0x03DD80(T, rec+8):
     rec+7 = 0;  rec+0 = 0x063DFC[[7E51]];  rec+1 = bit j of [7D80] (set or clear);  rec+3 = 0;
     0x03D582(rec);  0x03D50E(rec);  0x009D48(k), sub+1 = 0, sub+12 = sub+16;
     handler B = long 0x063E0C + 16 * MODE (0x03E26E MODE 0 / 0x03E0BA MODE 1) with (rec, d);
     rec+2 = 0 if its result's low byte == 1, else 2.
  rec+1 is NOT tested here: a suppressed record still gets 0x03D582 (fresh counts in rec+24) and handler B.  What
  handler B, 0x009D48 and 0x03D50E do is not decoded, so whether that leads to a pulse is NOT established.
EVIDENCE
  03DB26..03DB42 the angle; 03DB40..03DB4E the copies; 03DB58..03DBD4 the record loop.  emulated: pass S1 (150 cases,
  600 records, 305 of them with the suppression byte set after the refresh; handlers B / 0x009D48 / 0x03D50E /
  0x03D582 as logged stubs: the call sequence 0x03D582, 0x03D50E, 0x009D48, handler B per record and every record byte
  compared).
LISTING

==== FUNCTION 0x03DD8E .. 0x03DE6E  (112 instructions, 1 caller: 0x03D856 at 03D998) -- apply a MODE change now ====

CONTRACT  (r4 = T, r5 = the new MODE)
  for each record whose rec+0 differs from the new MODE, with d = 0x03DD80(T, rec+8):
     skip unless d > long [0x063E04 + 16 * new MODE] (= 100<<16 for both MODEs; signed);
     rec+2 == 0: rec+0 = new MODE;
     rec+2 == 1: handler B of the OLD MODE (rec, d); if its low byte == 1 and the sub-record is neither in state 1
                 with byte [429B + 28k] == 0 nor in state 2: 0x009D48(k), sub+1 = 0, rec+2 = 0, rec+0 = new MODE;
     other:      nothing.
  So a change of byte [726C] reaches an idle record more than 100 deg from its phase at once, not only at its re-arm.

==== FUNCTION 0x03DE6E .. 0x03DFBE  (131 instructions, 1 caller: 0x03D856 at 03D9B4) -- apply a [7D80] change now ====

CONTRACT  (r4 = T, r5 = the new mask, u16 [7D80])
  for each record j, with b = new mask & (1 << rec+12) and d = 0x03DD80(T, rec+8) (skip if d < 0):
     SUPPRESS    (rec+1 == 0, b set):    rec+2 == 0 -> rec+1 = 1;
                                         rec+2 == 1 -> if the sub-record is neither in state 1 with [429B+28k] == 0
                                                       nor in state 2: 0x009D48(k), rec+2 = 0, rec+1 = 1;
                                         other      -> nothing
     UN-SUPPRESS (rec+1 == 1, b clear):  rec+2 == 1 -> rec+1 = 0;
                                         rec+2 == 0 -> handler B(rec, d): low byte == 1 ? rec+1 = 0 : rec+2 = 2;
                                         other      -> nothing
     anything else (the bit agrees with rec+1, or rec+1 holds another value): nothing.
  So a [7D80] bit change is applied at once on these paths, not only at the record's re-arm.
EVIDENCE
  03DE86..03DEAA the four cases; 03DEAC..03DED2; 03DF20..03DF7E suppress; 03DF80..03DF9C un-suppress.  emulated:
  pass S2 (400 cases over rec+1 / rec+2 / sub+1 / [429B+28k] / the handler result / the mask / the tooth: every record
  byte and the call sequence compared; 338 immediate rec+1 changes).

==== FUNCTION 0x03DFC8 .. 0x03E0BA  (121 instructions; handler A of MODE 1, table word 0x063E18) ====

  else, by the state byte sub+1:
    1 (in flight): sub+20 == 0 -> rec+20 = rec+24; 0x009C38(k, rec+20)
                   sub+20 != 0 -> byte [429B + 28k] != 0 ? (0x009D48(k); sub+1 = 0) : (0x009C38(k, 0); sub+1 = 2)
    0 (idle):      sub+20 != 0 -> nothing.  Else, if byte rec+2 != 0 and !(r11 >= 0x03E0FA(sub)): sub+1 = 2.
                   Otherwise rec+20 = rec+24;  0x009B2C(k, r5 = rec+20, fr4 = (30*[7E54] - 10 - min(sub+12 - r11, 0))
                   mod 720 degrees, r6 = 0);  sub+1 = 1
    other:         nothing
  then byte [7E50] = 1 (0x03DD46), byte rec+2 = 1, byte rec+6 = 0.     k = byte rec+16 (= j).
  So the count value the driver gets is exactly rec+24 = trunc(4 * PW_j).  The idle-path test at 03E058 calls
  0x03E0FA (section below), which adds the calibration long 0x0CBDB4 itself, not [42F4].
EVIDENCE
  emulated: pass P (144 cases over every state with 0x009B2C / 0x009C38 / 0x009D48 stubbed and their arguments

==== FUNCTION 0x009B2C .. 0x009BFC  (104 instructions, 1 caller: 0x03DFC8) -- driver: accept a pulse ====

CONTRACT  (r4 = k, r5 = counts P, fr4 = angle A, r6 = flag; in a critical section, mask 0x10)
  span  = float(P + [4280] + [42F4]) * 180.0 / float([4158])
  a     = A - [4168], wrapped: + 720 if -60.0 > a; unchanged if 660.0 > a; - 720 otherwise
  start = a - span
  flag != 0 and 0.0 > start: nothing stored.  Else drec_k+0 = P, drec_k+8 = span, drec_k+4 = start, byte +23 = 2;
  if 35.0 > start: 0x009F72(k) and byte +23 = 0 (armed now; otherwise left pending).
  The width is NOT computed here; [42F4] (30800 after init, 0 before 0x03D640 runs) enters only the span (the start
  angle), never the width.

==== FUNCTION 0x009F72 .. 0x00A1A6  (238 instructions, 4 callers: 0x009A72, 0x009B2C, 0x009C38, 0x009F1C) -- arm ====

CONTRACT  (r4 = k; mask = 1 << k from 0x011A7C + 12k + 8)
  u16 drec_k+20 = (([416C] >> 4) + off) mod 2^16, where off = 0 if 0.0 > start angle, else s = trunc(float([4154]) *
                 start / 30) >> 4 (arithmetic), replaced by 0x7FFF only when its LOW 16 bits exceed 0x7FFF (00A008
                 extu.w, 00A00A cmp/gt): s = 0x12345 is added unclamped, so this is NOT min(s, 0x7FFF) for s >= 0x10000
  drec_k+12 = drec_k+0 (= P)
  in a critical section (0x0BE81C, mask 0xE0), on EVERY call:
    u16 [0xFFFFF444 + 2k] = u16 [0xFFFFF440] - 1;  u16 [0xFFFFF66A] = ~mask
    status bit clear (u16 [0xFFFFF666] & mask == 0):
        if (drec_k+20 - ([F440] + 3)) mod 2^16 >= 0x8000: drec_k+20 = [F440] + 3       (start already passed)
        u16 [0xFFFFF444 + 2k] = drec_k+20                                                 (COMPARE)
        0x00A4E0(k, (P + [4280]) >> 4)                                                    (WIDTH)
        byte drec_k+22 = 0
    status bit set (a pulse already running on channel k) -- the compare register keeps [F440] - 1; drec_k+20 is
    never written to it:  remaining R = u16 [0xFFFFF640+2k] + [4294+28k];
        d = (drec_k+20 - [F440]) mod 2^16 (0 if >= 0x8000); d > R: 0x00E6E4(0xFFFFF66C, mask, 1), byte +24 = 1;
        else width W = byte +22 == 1 ? (P >> 4) + R : ((P + [4280]) >> 4) +/- the signed offset d (floor 0);
        0x00A4E0(k, W); u16 [0xFFFFF666] = mask; byte +22 = 0
  So for a fresh pulse (status bit clear) the width given to the hardware is (counts + latency raw) >> 4.
EVIDENCE
  emulated: pass N (300 cases, MMIO served and every peripheral write captured and compared in order, both status
  states; the 'clear' / 'late' / 'active' paths 100 / 69 / 131).  ">> 4" is four shar (arithmetic).  Pass N2:
  diff 0x7FFF kept / 0x8000 and 0xFFFF moved; [F440] = 0xFFFE -> compare writes 0xFFFD then 0x0001; status set ->
  exactly one compare write ([F440] - 1); s = 0x12345 / 0x7FFF added, 0x8000 / 0x18000 capped; P = 0xFFFFFFF0 with
  L = 0 -> w = -1 -> register 0xFFFF twice and [4294 + 28k] = 0xFFFF0000.

==== FUNCTION 0x00A4E0 .. 0x00A55E (40 instructions, 7 callers) and 0x011848 .. 0x011874 (22, 5 callers) -- the write ====

CONTRACT
  0x00A4E0(r4 = k, r5 = w): [4294 + 28k] = (w > 0xFFFF unsigned) ? w - 0xFFFF : 0;
                            0x011848(r4 = long 0x011A7C + 12k = 0xFFFFF640 + 2k, r5 = min(w, 0xFFFF))
  The compare is UNSIGNED (00A4F0 cmp/hi): a negative w (counts + latency < 0 before the arithmetic >> 4) gives the
  maximum register 0xFFFF and an overflow w - 0xFFFF mod 2^32.  Whether PW can be negative is not established (RESET
  value [72DC], lower clamp [733C]).  0x00A4E0 has seven callers (census); only the 0x009F72 / 0x009C38 uses are read.
  0x011848(r4 = address, r5 = value): in a critical section (0x0BE81C, mask 0xE0) stores u16 r5 to @r4 TWICE.
  0x00A55E(k) reads it back: u16 [0xFFFFF640 + 2k] + [4294 + 28k].
EVIDENCE
  emulated: pass M (44 cases, k = 0..3, w = 0 .. 0xFFFF / 0x10000 / 66320 / 0x7FFFFFFF / 0x80000000 / 0xFFFFFFFF:
  the two MMIO stores and the overflow cell exact); pass N2 (a negative w through 0x009F72).

      max-like floor against [728C] for EVERY channel, its flag 0 or not (02F17A..02F1E4 test no flag), decayed by
      [7274] = T(D, [6354]) per pass (one of eight records by bytes [90C1] / [3158] / [72D1]; factors 0.85..0.96);
      RESET to [72DC] when byte [726C] != 0 (02EFD2..02F21E; passes A, V1).  Byte [72D1] is a latch set at 02EFE8 and
      cleared only by 0x02F83E (02F85C), which runs at init (00E10A -> 047F04 -> 02EF08 -> 02F888): after init [726C] =
      1, the four flags are 1 and [72D1] = 0 (pass U) -- and at run time from task-9 runner 0x04907C (0491B0 ->
      0x02EF0C -> 02EF1A) whenever byte [65F6] == 1 (pass V2).
  E2  0x038DC0 writes [7B6C + 4j] = T2(PIPWC A..D, [7324], [6624]); the definitions name the four data pointers

      the factors (0.898..1.086) are the per-channel multipliers of 0x0301E4 (038DC0..038E02; pass B).

  E4  0x037B74 forms S4 = [7AB4] = clamp(1 + [7ABC]*[7AC0]*[7AC4]*[7AC8], 0.5, 1.5) or 1.0.  [7ABC] (AD71C through

      0x03CD00, caller 0x047C40; pass V4).  None of them enters the pulse of the same pass; [7D5C] / [7D64] feed
      0x03CD4A's [7D68] (03CF46..03CF54), which the A/F-learning pass copies to [7874] (03457E..034586) -- a
      later-pass effect on [7878] is NOT established (N10).
  E7  0x03D582 writes rec_j+24 = trunc([7290 + 4 ch] * [7DA4] / 0.25), ch = byte rec_j+12 = j (0x03D708 at 03D77C)
      in MODE 1, and from 0x02F9E0 in MODE 0 (pass G).  MODE = byte rec+0 is latched: written at the re-arm (03DA20),
      by 0x03DB16 (03DB6E) and by 0x03DD8E (03DE4E), each from 0x063DFC[0x03D4F6()] = 0 if byte [726C] == 1 else 1
      (passes L, S1, S3, S4).
  E8  [7DA4] = 1.0 (0x03D68C, called at 04A9C8 just before 0x03D856 at 04A9CE) (pass I).
  E9  0x03DFC8 copies rec+24 to rec+20 and passes it as r5 to 0x009B2C (idle) or 0x009C38 (in flight) with
      k = byte rec+16 = j (pass P).
  E10 0x009B2C stores drec_k+0 = P and arms through 0x009F72 when its start angle is below 35 degrees (pass O).  [42F4]
      is written by 0x009E44 (30800 through 0x03D640) and by 0x009860 (0, earlier at init) and read only by 0x009B2C
      and 0x009EA0, both in the angle span; 0x03E0FA adds long [0x0CBDB4] itself; none of them feeds the width
      (literal scan; passes O, S5, U).
  E11 0x009F72 writes u16 [0xFFFFF444 + 2k] = [F440] - 1 on every call.  Only when status bit k of u16 [F666] is clear
      does it then write the compare value drec_k+20 (moved to [F440] + 3 when (drec+20 - ([F440] + 3)) mod 2^16 >=
      0x8000) and call 0x00A4E0(k, (P + [4280]) >> 4); with the bit set the register keeps [F440] - 1.  The start
      offset is replaced by 0x7FFF only when its LOW 16 bits exceed 0x7FFF (00A008..00A018) (passes N, N2).
  E12 0x00A4E0 / 0x011848 store min(w, 0xFFFF) (unsigned compare) twice as u16 to 0xFFFFF640 + 2k and keep w - 0xFFFF
      in [4294 + 28k]; a negative w gives 0xFFFF (00A4F0; passes M, N2).  Register addresses come from the ROM table
      0x011A7C.
  E13 0x0303C0 writes u16 [7350] = [4280] = "Injector Latency_"([4130], [6210]) and [734C] = that / 4 (pass J); the
      latency is flat across [6210]: 12588 / 6857 / 4499 / 3225 / 2693.
  E14 End to end with every function real (pass R, 262 cases, S4 in {0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.3}): for a fresh MODE-1 pulse
      armed with the status bit clear, u16 [0xFFFFF640 + 2j] = min((trunc(4 * [7290 + 4j]) + [4280]) >> 4, 0xFFFF),
      written twice after the two compare writes, with [7290 + 4j] = clamp([7310 + 4j] * (S4 * C) * [7B6C + 4j] *
      [7BC4], [733C], 262136).  0 <= 4*PW + L - 16*register < 16 while 4*PW + L <= 0xFFFFF; at PW = 262136 the
      register is 0xFFFF with 167 / 785 left in [4294 + 28j] for L = 2693 / 12588.
  E15 0x05D478 (getter index 32) returns clamp(trunc([7D84] / 256 + 0.5), 0, 255) with [7D84] = 0.25 * rec_0+20 +
      [734C] (0x03D648, 0x05D478; passes H, K).  That it is the logged P21 rests on index = paramid (N8).
  E16 In 0x03D856's per-record loop a record with byte rec+1 != 0 gets neither 0x03D582 nor handler A (03DAB2..03DAB6,
      03DAD4..03DAD8); 0x03D648 then zeroes its [7D84 + 4j] / [7D94 + 4j].  rec+1 = bit j of u16 [7D80] is written,
      set or clear, at the re-arm (03DA3A), by 0x03DB16 (03DB88) and, on a change of [7D80], at once by 0x03DE6E
      under the conditions in its CONTRACT (passes S1, S2, S4).
  E17 Every code and calibration byte used (13,201 bytes, the audit's ranges included) is identical in stock, 20.19c and
      20.19e (pass Q).
  E18 0x03DB16 (called at 03D97C when the tooth step != 1) runs 0x03D582 and handler B (0x03E26E / 0x03E0BA) for EVERY
      record without testing rec+1 (03DB90, 03DBC6; pass S1).
  E19 0x03D856 re-arms a record only when the tooth angle equals its phase (0x03DD80 result >= 720<<16, 03D9FA).  On
      the rec+2 == 0 path it skips while byte [429B + 28k] != 0, sets rec+2 = 2 when the distance is <= 100 deg, and
      counts u16 [7E48] before the suppression test; on the rec+2 == 1 path it needs byte rec+6 == 1 (pass S4).
  E20 0x009860 (init, 00DFFE, before 0x047C40 at 00E10A) writes 0 to every width register 0xFFFFF640 + 2k (twice
      each), to [4280] and to [42F4] (pass U).
  E21 (audit) [733C] = 0.0 if byte [641C] == 1, else [0x0CBE08] = 300.0, written by 0x02FF8E (02FFA0) at 02FF7E right
      before 0x0301E4 (tail branch 02FF8A) in 0x02FF7C; no other writer found.  So the COPY / FLOOR-path PW is >= 0.0
      (>= 300.0 unless [641C] == 1; a NaN product gives [733C]); only the RESET pass can store a negative PW, from
      [72DC] (pass V6; V5 runs the 300.0 clamp through to the register: PW 300 -> counts 1200 -> (1200 + 2693) >> 4 =
      243).
  E22 (audit) [7324]'s third reader 0x0345A4 (A/F-learning pass 0x03452A, 03456C) sets byte [7883] = 1 only if nine
      terms hold, one of them [7324] > [733C] (034756 F695, strict, NaN fails): false exactly when [7324] is on its
      lower clamp (034636..034776; pass V3).
  E23 (audit) 0x02F83E is reachable at run time: 0x04907C (task 9) runs straight from 0490E8 (byte [8EDC] == 0) to
      0491B0 jsr 0x02EF0C, and 0x02EF0C calls 0x02F83E iff byte [65F6] == 1 (02EF10..02EF1A; pass V2, all 256 values).
  E24 (audit) In 0x04AA58 order (0x038DC0 at 04AAEC, 0x0397AC at 04AAF8, 0x02FF7C -> 0x0301E4 at 04AB16) the trims
      and F of a pass are built from the previous pass's [7324] / [7344]; run with every function real over two passes,
      incl. F != 1 and the 300.0 clamp, through to the register (pass V5).

==== NOT ESTABLISHED (and what would settle it) ====
  N1  Which physical cylinder record j / channel j drives.  Record j has phase 0 / 360 / 180 / 540 degrees (0x063E20)
      and driver channel j; settling it needs the crank-angle origin (the writer of [7E54] / the reference tooth) or
      the board wiring of the four outputs.  The A/B/C/D -> cylinder 1-4 order is NOT shown by these bytes.
  N2  The SH7058 module and channel names of 0xFFFFF440 / F444..F44A / F640..F646 / F666 / F66A / F66C.  Needs the
      hardware manual's register map (no copy cited here).
  N3  The time unit.  "PW in us, register LSB = 4 us" rests on two definition scalings ([7324]'s axis x*.001 ms and
      latency x*.00025 ms), consistent with the bytes (4*PW is added to the latency raw count) but not derived from
      the timer's clock / prescaler, which was not read.  Also what consumes the excess kept in [4294 + 28j] when the
      register saturates (0x00A55E reads it back for 0x009F72, 0x009C38, 0x00A280, 0x00A470; the timer side is not
      traced).
  N4  0x009C38 (in-flight update) and 0x00A280 (the 0x03DCB6 asynchronous path) are not emulated; 0x009EA0 is read in
      part only; 0x009F1C (deferred arming), 0x00E6E4, 0x009D48, 0x03D50E, the MODE-0 handler A 0x03E168 and both
      handlers B 0x03E26E / 0x03E0BA are not decoded.  So what 0x03DB16 does for a SUPPRESSED record (it runs 0x03D582
      and handler B, E18) is not established: whether a pulse can follow needs handler B decoded.
  N5  The MODE-0 pulse inputs (0x02F974, 0x02FCBA, 0x02FB1C, 0x02FDE0) are not decoded.
  N6  The value of [7AC0] before 0x037E70 first writes it, and of bytes [8374] / [8375] before 0x04BDAA first writes
      them: no initialiser was found (writers, a lower bound: 0x037E70 only; 0x04BDAA only), and a reset-time bulk RAM
      initialisation, if any, was not traced (reset vector 0x000C0C not followed).  S4 = 1.0 needs [7AC0] finite.
      The meaning of 0x037E70's byte conditions ([7AD1] [7AD3] [7ADC]) is not established.
  N7  The source of the 0x03DCB6 pulse (a message record handled by 0x04AF88 / 0x04AF9A) is not traced; none of the
      readers of [7290..729C] / [7320..7344] found posts it (lower bound).
  N8  Index 32 = paramid 0x20: the writer of u16 [8F28] was not decoded (consistent with indices 8 / 9, not proof).
  N9  The meaning of AC760, AD71C, AC634, AC648, AE774 / AE788 and the eight decay records: no definition names their
      data.
  N10 Whether [7324] or [7338] reaches a LATER pulse through the A/F-learning pass 0x03452A, reported (not verified
      here) to write [7878], a term of C: [7324] reaches byte [7883] (E22) and [7338] reaches [7874] through [7D5C] /
      [7D64] -> 0x03CD4A -> [7D68] (0x03D23A not decoded).  Settle: decode 0x03452A's callees (0x034778, 0x034884,
      0x034B1C, 0x034C18, 0x034CC8, 0x034CEE, 0x034D52) for their use of [7883] / [7874] and their writes of [7878].  Also not followed: 0x027132 and 0x03CFDA (other
      readers of the lags), the other ~20 readers of [7D68], [8368] / [8369] beyond 0x02FE7E, 0x07D620's status words.
  N11 The writers of [4158] [4154] [416C] [4168] [4167] (period / angle cells) are not decoded; they move the start,
      not the width.
  N12 Power-on contents of [7290..729C], [728C], and rec_j / drec_k beyond the 0x03D708 / 0x009860 init; whether PW
      can be negative is now narrowed to the RESET pass (value [72DC], written by 0x02F974, not decoded): the lower
      clamp [733C] is 0.0 or 300.0 (E21) -- a negative counts + latency would give the maximum register 0xFFFF (E12).
  N14 (audit) When byte [65F6] is 1 after start, i.e. whether the run-time 0x02F83E call (E23) ever fires while the
      engine runs: [65F6] = byte [415C] (0x022F1C at 022F30, callers 0x047C40 and 0x048E16), zeroed by 0x022DB0 at
      022DC6 (caller 0x04A94C); the writer of [415C] and the rate of 0x048E16 are not decoded.
  N13 The [7E55] == 1 initialisation block of 0x03D856 (03D89E..03D972), and what writes byte rec+6, byte [429B + 28k]
      and byte [7E55].

==== REVIEW HISTORY (2026-11-27: tracer -> adversarial reviewer -> this third, deciding decode) ====
  Each disputed claim, who was right, and the bytes / emulation that decided it.  "Reviewer right" means the
  correction was checked here from the bytes before it was applied.  Part of three reviewer statements did not
  survive -- C28, C29 and missed item 2, all the same error (it overlooked 0x03DD8E / 0x03DE6E).

  C3  0x009F72 compare register.  REVIEWER RIGHT (imprecise).  [F440] - 1 is written on every call; drec_k+20 reaches
      the register only on the status-clear path:
      00A02A: 996B  mov.w @(0x00A104),r9          ; r9 = 0xFFFFF440 (sign-extended mov.w)
      00A030: 353C  add r3,r5                     ; r5 = [F440] + 0xFFFF
      00A044: 2151  mov.w r5,@r1                  ; u16 [F444 + 2k] = [F440] - 1, before any branch
      00A05C: 2108  tst r0,r1                     ; T = (([F666] & mask) == 0)
      00A05E: 8D72  bt/s 0x00A146                 ; status clear -> 00A146
      00A17C: 2301  mov.w r0,@r3                  ; u16 [F444 + 2k] = drec_k+20: reached only from 00A146
      emulated pass N2: diff 0x7FFF kept, 0x8000 moved; [F440] = 0xFFFE -> 0xFFFD then 0x0001; status set -> the
      single write 0x00FF = [F440] - 1.  Repaired: 0x009F72 CONTRACT, E11.

  C4  End-to-end formula.  REVIEWER RIGHT (imprecise), and the old pass R was not end to end (it wrote driver +0 and
      start = -1.0 by hand, fixed [7AB4] = 1.0).  It holds for a fresh MODE-1 pulse armed with the status bit clear;
      counts arriving in flight go elsewhere:
      03DFF2: 8801  cmp/eq #1,r0                  ; T = (sub+1 == 1: in flight)
      03E012: 420B  jsr @r2                       ; in flight: 0x009C38, not 0x009B2C
      Pass R is rewritten: REAL 0x0301E4 -> 0x02EFD2 -> 0x03D68C -> 0x03D582 -> 0x03DFC8 -> 0x009B2C -> 0x009F72 ->
      0x00A4E0 -> 0x011848, 262 cases, S4 in {0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.3}, plus PW edges.  Repaired: ANSWER, E14.

  C10 [42F4].  REVIEWER RIGHT.  0x009860 is a second writer and 0x009EA0 a second reader; 0x03E0FA reads the
      calibration long, not [42F4]:
      009862: EE00  mov #0,r14                    ; r14 = 0
      009926: 22E2  mov.l r14,@r2                 ; long [42F4] = 0 (pointer from 009924)
      009ED0: 6312  mov.l @r1,r3                  ; r3 = [42F4] in 0x009EA0 (pointer from 009ECC)
      03E108: 420B  jsr @r2                       ; -> 0x03D634, which returns long [0x0CBDB4] (its rts slot 03D638)
      03D638: 6022  mov.l @r2,r0                  ; r0 = long [0x0CBDB4] (r2 = 0x0CBDB4 from 03D634)
      find_writers' extra GBR hits 008618 / 008760 are the pool word 0xC2B40000 (f32 -90.0), not code.  Pass S5:
      0x03E0FA's result is the same with [42F4] garbage or never written.  Repaired: 0x0303C0 CONTRACT, RAM cells,
      CALIBRATION, E10, X8.

  C21 0x04BDAA flag pair.  REVIEWER RIGHT.  The fall-through is for every byte other than 1 / 2 / 3:
      04BF0A: 6050  mov.b @r5,r0                  ; sign-extending byte load of 0x0D6010
      04BF0C: 8801  cmp/eq #1,r0                  ; == 1 -> (1,1)
      04BF12: 8802  cmp/eq #2,r0                  ; == 2 -> (0,1)
      04BF18: 8803  cmp/eq #3,r0                  ; == 3 -> (1,0)
      04BF1C: C41C  mov.b @(28,gbr),r0            ; anything else: the [8374] / [8375] test
      emulated pass E: 4 / 0x7F / 0x81 / 0xFF give exactly the writes of 0; the window is inclusive (+-2^-10 -> 0.0,
      one ulp outside -> 90.0, NaN -> 0.0); k = [0x0D602C] = 0x3F7D70A3 = 0.98999995.  Repaired: 0x04BDAA CONTRACT,
      CALIBRATION, E5.

  C28 Record MODE.  BOTH PARTLY RIGHT.  The reviewer is right that rec+0 is a latched per-record byte written at the
      re-arm and in 0x03DB16 (the tracer's statement ignored the latch).  The reviewer is WRONG that a change of
      [726C] "reaches a record only at its next re-arm": 0x03D856 calls 0x03DD8E as soon as 0x03D4F6's result
      changes, and 0x03DD8E stores the new MODE at once:
      03DA20: 2D20  mov.b r2,@r13                 ; rec+0 at the re-arm
      03DB6E: 2B20  mov.b r2,@r11                 ; rec+0 in 0x03DB16
      03D990: 8906  bt 0x03D9A0                   ; unchanged -> skip
      03D998: B1F9  bsr 0x03DD8E                  ; changed -> 0x03DD8E(T, new MODE)
      03DE4E: 2DB0  mov.b r11,@r13                ; rec+0 = new MODE, immediately
      emulated pass S3: 328 immediate MODE changes in 400 cases (idle records more than 100 deg from the phase, and
      scheduled ones when the old MODE's handler B returns 1 and the sub-record can be cancelled).  Repaired: ANSWER,
      0x03D582 / 0x03D4F6 CONTRACTs, E7, new 0x03DD8E section.

  C29 Suppression byte rec+1.  BOTH PARTLY RIGHT.  The reviewer is right that the bit sets OR clears rec+1, and that
      0x03DB16 runs 0x03D582 and handler B for every record regardless of rec+1:
      03DA3A: 80D1  mov.b r0,@(1,r13)             ; rec+1 = bit j at the re-arm
      03DB88: 80B1  mov.b r0,@(1,r11)             ; rec+1 = bit j in 0x03DB16
      03DB90: 420B  jsr @r2                       ; 0x03D582 for every record: no rec+1 test in 0x03DB16
      03DBC6: 420B  jsr @r2                       ; handler B for every record
      The reviewer is WRONG that rec+1 changes "only when record j re-arms or in 0x03DB16": a [7D80] change is applied
      at once by 0x03DE6E:
      03D9B4: B25B  bsr 0x03DE6E                  ; changed [7D80] -> 0x03DE6E(T, new mask)
      03DF7E: 80D1  mov.b r0,@(1,r13)             ; rec+1 = 1 (r0 = 1 from 03DF7A), suppressed now
      03DF9C: 80D1  mov.b r0,@(1,r13)             ; rec+1 = 0 (r0 = 0 from 03DF9A), un-suppressed now
      emulated passes S1 (0x03DB16, 305 suppressed records still get 0x03D582 and handler B), S2 (338 immediate rec+1
      changes in 400 cases), S4.  Repaired: ANSWER item (4), E16, E18, new 0x03DB16 / 0x03DE6E sections.

  C31 Logged IPW.  REVIEWER RIGHT.  The original evidence already said index = paramid is not proven; the statement
      now makes the identity conditional on it.  The index is a RAM word read at run time:
      05F72E: 6641  mov.w @r4,r6                  ; r6 = u16 [8F28] (writer not decoded)
      Repaired: ANSWER, 0x05D478 CONTRACT, E15 (N8 unchanged).

  C35 Units.  REVIEWER RIGHT.  "Within 4 us" holds only below saturation and only if both definition scalings are
      right.  The register is 0xFFFF once 4*PW + L > 0xFFFFF, the excess going to [4294 + 28j]:
      00A4F0: 3E46  cmp/hi r4,r14                 ; T = (w > 0xFFFF) unsigned
      emulated pass R: 0 <= 4*PW + L - 16*register < 16 below 0xFFFFF; PW = 262136 -> 0xFFFF with 167 / 785 for L =
      2693 / 12588.  Repaired: ANSWER, E14, N3.
